{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JTE2icVPNdinn54DhUqjW5","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n2\tpragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n3\t\n4\t// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n5\timport \"forge-std/console.sol\";\n6\t\n7\t// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n8\t// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n12\t * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n13\t * @author BuidlGuidl\n14\t */\n15\tcontract YourContract {\n16\t  // State Variables\n17\t  address public immutable owner;\n18\t  string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n19\t  bool public premium = false;\n20\t  uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n21\t  mapping(address => uint256) public userGreetingCounter;\n22\t\n23\t  // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n24\t  event GreetingChange(\n25\t    address indexed greetingSetter,\n26\t    string newGreeting,\n27\t    bool premium,\n28\t    uint256 value\n29\t  );\n30\t\n31\t  // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n32\t  // Check packages/foundry/deploy/Deploy.s.sol\n33\t  constructor(address _owner) {\n34\t    owner = _owner;\n35\t  }\n36\t\n37\t  // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n38\t  // Check the withdraw() function\n39\t  modifier isOwner() {\n40\t    // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n41\t    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n42\t    _;\n43\t  }\n44\t\n45\t  /**\n46\t   * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n47\t   *\n48\t   * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n49\t   */\n50\t  function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n51\t    // Print data to the anvil chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n52\t\n53\t    console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n54\t    console.logString(_newGreeting);\n55\t\n56\t    greeting = _newGreeting;\n57\t    totalCounter += 1;\n58\t    userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n59\t\n60\t    // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n61\t    if (msg.value > 0) {\n62\t      premium = true;\n63\t    } else {\n64\t      premium = false;\n65\t    }\n66\t\n67\t    // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n68\t    emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n69\t  }\n70\t\n71\t  /**\n72\t   * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n73\t   * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n74\t   */\n75\t  function withdraw() public isOwner {\n76\t    (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n77\t    require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n78\t  }\n79\t\n80\t  /**\n81\t   * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n82\t   */\n83\t  receive() external payable { }\n84\t}\n85\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"d21ae5c7-e432-4737-9396-063708bfbea4","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:53:29.642Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T165312Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/foundry/contracts/YourContract.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\nimport \"forge-std/console.sol\";\n\n// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n\n/**\n * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract YourContract {\n  // State Variables\n  address public immutable owner;\n  string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n  bool public premium = false;\n  uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n  mapping(address => uint256) public userGreetingCounter;\n\n  // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n  event GreetingChange(\n    address indexed greetingSetter,\n    string newGreeting,\n    bool premium,\n    uint256 value\n  );\n\n  // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n  // Check packages/foundry/deploy/Deploy.s.sol\n  constructor(address _owner) {\n    owner = _owner;\n  }\n\n  // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n  // Check the withdraw() function\n  modifier isOwner() {\n    // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n    _;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n   *\n   * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n   */\n  function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n    // Print data to the anvil chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n\n    console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n    console.logString(_newGreeting);\n\n    greeting = _newGreeting;\n    totalCounter += 1;\n    userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n\n    // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n    if (msg.value > 0) {\n      premium = true;\n    } else {\n      premium = false;\n    }\n\n    // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n    emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n   * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n   */\n  function withdraw() public isOwner {\n    (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n    require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n   */\n  receive() external payable { }\n}\n","numLines":85,"startLine":1,"totalLines":85}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012nniHraYBqnaEfcDWGSjLN","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n2\tpragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n3\t\n4\timport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\n5\timport { DeployYourContract } from \"./DeployYourContract.s.sol\";\n6\t\n7\t/**\n8\t * @notice Main deployment script for all contracts\n9\t * @dev Run this when you want to deploy multiple contracts at once\n10\t *\n11\t * Example: yarn deploy # runs this script(without`--file` flag)\n12\t */\n13\tcontract DeployScript is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n14\t  function run() external {\n15\t    // Deploys all your contracts sequentially\n16\t    // Add new deployments here when needed\n17\t\n18\t    \n19\t    DeployYourContract deployYourContract = new DeployYourContract();\n20\t    deployYourContract.run();\n21\t\n22\t\n23\t    // Deploy another contract\n24\t    // DeployMyContract myContract = new DeployMyContract();\n25\t    // myContract.run();\n26\t  }\n27\t}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"4b18eab2-bb06-4f9f-8616-08d9f623abad","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:53:30.706Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T165312Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/foundry/script/Deploy.s.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport { DeployYourContract } from \"./DeployYourContract.s.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Main deployment script for all contracts\n * @dev Run this when you want to deploy multiple contracts at once\n *\n * Example: yarn deploy # runs this script(without`--file` flag)\n */\ncontract DeployScript is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n  function run() external {\n    // Deploys all your contracts sequentially\n    // Add new deployments here when needed\n\n    \n    DeployYourContract deployYourContract = new DeployYourContract();\n    deployYourContract.run();\n\n\n    // Deploy another contract\n    // DeployMyContract myContract = new DeployMyContract();\n    // myContract.run();\n  }\n}","numLines":27,"startLine":1,"totalLines":27}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MzGvyxkTxwAbmUKhPBQuaH","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n2\tpragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n3\t\n4\timport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\n5\timport \"../contracts/YourContract.sol\";\n6\t\n7\t/**\n8\t * @notice Deploy script for YourContract contract\n9\t * @dev Inherits ScaffoldETHDeploy which:\n10\t *      - Includes forge-std/Script.sol for deployment\n11\t *      - Includes ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier\n12\t *      - Provides `deployer` variable\n13\t * Example:\n14\t * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol  # local anvil chain\n15\t * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol --network optimism # live network (requires keystore)\n16\t */\n17\tcontract DeployYourContract is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n18\t    /**\n19\t     * @dev Deployer setup based on `ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT` in `.env`:\n20\t     *      - \"scaffold-eth-default\": Uses Anvil's account #9 (0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720), no password prompt\n21\t     *      - \"scaffold-eth-custom\": requires password used while creating keystore\n22\t     *\n23\t     * Note: Must use ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier to:\n24\t     *      - Setup correct `deployer` account and fund it\n25\t     *      - Export contract addresses & ABIs to `nextjs` packages\n26\t     */\n27\t    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n28\t        new YourContract(deployer);\n29\t    }\n30\t}\n31\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"53ec7bdc-bb85-489c-b5d7-78af0c1a008f","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:53:31.813Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T165312Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/foundry/script/DeployYourContract.s.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/YourContract.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Deploy script for YourContract contract\n * @dev Inherits ScaffoldETHDeploy which:\n *      - Includes forge-std/Script.sol for deployment\n *      - Includes ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier\n *      - Provides `deployer` variable\n * Example:\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol  # local anvil chain\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol --network optimism # live network (requires keystore)\n */\ncontract DeployYourContract is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    /**\n     * @dev Deployer setup based on `ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT` in `.env`:\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-default\": Uses Anvil's account #9 (0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720), no password prompt\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-custom\": requires password used while creating keystore\n     *\n     * Note: Must use ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier to:\n     *      - Setup correct `deployer` account and fund it\n     *      - Export contract addresses & ABIs to `nextjs` packages\n     */\n    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n        new YourContract(deployer);\n    }\n}\n","numLines":31,"startLine":1,"totalLines":31}}}

vm.stopBroadcast();\n55\t    }\n56\t\n57\t    function exportDeployments() internal {\n58\t        // fetch already existing contracts\n59\t        root = vm.projectRoot();\n60\t        path = MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport { Script, console } from \"forge-std/Script.sol\";\nimport { Vm } from \"forge-std/Vm.sol\";\n\ncontract ScaffoldETHDeploy is Script {\n    error InvalidChain();\n    error DeployerHasNoBalance();\n    error InvalidPrivateKey(string);\n\n    event AnvilSetBalance(address account, uint256 amount);\n    event FailedAnvilRequest();\n\n    struct Deployment {\n        string name;\n        address addr;\n    }\n\n    string root;\n    string path;\n    Deployment[] public deployments;\n    uint256 constant ANVIL_BASE_BALANCE = 10000 ether;\n\n    /// @notice The deployer address for every run\n    address deployer;\n\n    /// @notice Use this modifier on your run() function on your deploy scripts\n    modifier ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner() {\n        deployer = _startBroadcast();\n        if (deployer == address(0)) {\n            revert InvalidPrivateKey(\"Invalid private key\");\n        }\n        _;\n        _stopBroadcast();\n        exportDeployments();\n    }\n\n    function _startBroadcast() internal returns (address) {\n        vm.startBroadcast();\n        (, address _deployer,) = vm.readCallers();\n\n        if (block.chainid == 31337 && _deployer.balance == 0) {\n            try vm.deal(_deployer, ANVIL_BASE_BALANCE) {\n                emit AnvilSetBalance(_deployer, ANVIL_BASE_BALANCE);\n            } catch {\n                emit FailedAnvilRequest();\n            }\n        }\n        return _deployer;\n    }\n\n    function _stopBroadcast() internal {\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n    }\n\n    function exportDeployments() internal {\n        // fetch already existing contracts\n        root = vm.projectRoot();\n        path = string.concat(root, \"/deployments/\");\n        string memory chainIdStr = vm.toString(block.chainid);\n        path = string.concat(path, string.concat(chainIdStr, \".json\"));\n\n        string memory jsonWrite;\n\n        uint256 len = deployments.length;\n\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {\n            vm.serializeString(jsonWrite, vm.toString(deployments[i].addr), deployments[i].name);\n        }\n\n        string memory chainName;\n\n        try vm.getChain(block.chainid) returns (Vm.Chain memory chain) {\n            chainName = chain.name;\n        } catch {\n            chainName = findChainName();\n        }\n        jsonWrite = vm.serializeString(jsonWrite, \"networkName\", chainName);\n        vm.writeJson(jsonWrite, path);\n    }\n\n    function findChainName() public returns (string memory) {\n        uint256 thisChainId = block.chainid;\n        string[2][] memory allRpcUrls = vm.rpcUrls();\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < allRpcUrls.length; i++) {\n            try vm.createSelectFork(allRpcUrls[i][1]) {\n                if (block.chainid == thisChainId) {\n                    return allRpcUrls[i][0];\n                }\n            } catch {\n                continue;\n            }\n        }\n        revert InvalidChain();\n    }\n}\n","numLines":98,"startLine":1,"totalLines":98}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AFtUpb8MfpSWsbppZ2KXb2","type":"tool_result","content":".PHONY: build deploy generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork format lint test verify\n\nDEPLOY_SCRIPT ?= script/Deploy.s.sol\n\n# setup wallet for anvil\nsetup-anvil-wallet:\n\tshx rm ~/.foundry/keystores/scaffold-eth-default 2>/dev/null; \tshx rm -rf broadcast/Deploy.s.sol/31337\n\tcast wallet import --private-key 0x2a871d0798f97d79848a013d4936a73bf4cc922c825d33c1cf7073dff6d409c6 --unsafe-password 'localhost' scaffold-eth-default\n\n# Start local chain\nchain: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil\n\n# Start a fork\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337\n\n# Deploy the contracts\ndeploy:\n\t@if [ ! -f \"$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)\" ]; then \t\techo \"Error: Deploy script '$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)' not found\"; \t\texit 1; \tfi\n\t@if [ \"$(RPC_URL)\" = \"localhost\" ]; then \t\tif [ \"$(ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT)\" = \"scaffold-eth-default\" ]; then \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --password localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\telse \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\tfi \telse \t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --ffi; {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01LKAwk8Jg2CmUa9t2miMo6D","type":"tool_result","content":"checkAccountBalance.js\ngenerateKeystore.js\ngenerateTsAbis.js\nimportAccount.js\nlistKeystores.js\nparseArgs.js\nrevealPK.js\nselectOrCreateKeystore.js\n---env---\n# Template for foundry environment variables.\n\n# For local development, copy this file, rename it to .env, and fill in the values.\n\n# We provide default values so developers can start prototyping out of the box,\n# but we recommend getting your own API Keys for Production Apps.\n\n# DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY is used while deploying contract.\n# On anvil chain the value of it can be empty since we use the prefunded account\n# which comes with anvil chain to deploy contract.\n# NOTE: You don't need to manually change the value of DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY, it should\n# be auto filled when run `yarn generate`.\n\n# Alchemy rpc URL is used while deploying the contracts to some testnets/mainnets, checkout `foundry.toml` for it's use.\nALCHEMY_API_KEY=IZYEU2cWBgnFmgiTAgpWD\n# Etherscan API key is used to verify the contract on etherscan.\nETHERSCAN_API_KEY=DNXJA8RX2Q3VZ4URQIWP7Z68CJXQZSC6AW\n# Default account for localhost / use \"scaffold-eth-custom\" if you wish to use a generated account or imported account\nLOCALHOST_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT=scaffold-eth-default","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"a02557df-a649-43c4-a0fc-c56d486ac756","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:56:16.048Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"checkAccountBalance.js\ngenerateKeystore.js\ngenerateTsAbis.js\nimportAccount.js\nlistKeystores.js\nparseArgs.js\nrevealPK.js\nselectOrCreateKeystore.js\n---env---\n# Template for foundry environment variables.\n\n# For local development, copy this file, rename it to .env, and fill in the values.\n\n# We provide default values so developers can start prototyping out of the box,\n# but we recommend getting your own API Keys for Production Apps.\n\n# DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY is used while deploying contract.\n# On anvil chain the value of it can be empty since we use the prefunded account\n# which comes with anvil chain to deploy contract.\n# NOTE: You don't need to manually change the value of DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY, it should\n# be auto filled when run `yarn generate`.\n\n# Alchemy rpc URL is used while deploying the contracts to some testnets/mainnets, checkout `foundry.toml` for it's use.\nALCHEMY_API_KEY=IZYEU2cWBgnFmgiTAgpWD\n# Etherscan API key is used to verify the contract on etherscan.\nETHERSCAN_API_KEY=DNXJA8RX2Q3VZ4URQIWP7Z68CJXQZSC6AW\n# Default account for localhost / use \"scaffold-eth-custom\" if you wish to use a generated account or {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMEcMBSUJLmCUkUNp4sS","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Ngk6QQAryYwg3LYC9xk4FY","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T165312Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\nimport { IERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport { SafeERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice A tip jar denominated in a single ERC20 (USDC on Base).\n * @dev Tippers must `approve` the jar for the tip amount first, then call `tip`.\n *      The jar pulls the tokens with `transferFrom`, records the tip in an\n *      on-chain feed, and lets the owner withdraw the collected balance.\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    struct TipEntry {\n        address from;\n        uint256 amount;\n        string message;\n        uint256 timestamp;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice The ERC20 accepted as tips (Base USDC).\n    IERC20 public immutable token;\n\n    /// @notice The account allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n    address public immutable owner;\n\n    /// @notice Every tip ever received, oldest first.\n    TipEntry[] public tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total tipped per address (in token base units).\n    mapping(address => uint256) public totalTippedBy;\n\n    /// @notice Lifetime total of all tips received (in token base units).\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    event Tipped(address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    error NotOwner();\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    constructor(IERC20 _token, address _owner) {\n        token = _token;\n        owner = _owner;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a tip. Caller must have approved this contract for `amount` first.\n     * @param amount Tip amount in token base units (USDC has 6 decimals, so 1 USDC = 1_000_000).\n     * @param message A short public message shown in the tip feed.\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        // Pull the tokens first so we only record tips that actually settled.\n        token.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        tips.push(TipEntry({ from: msg.sender, amount: amount, message: message, timestamp: block.timestamp }));\n        totalTippedBy[msg.sender] += amount;\n        totalTipped += amount;\n\n        emit Tipped(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips in the feed.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Return the most recent `count` tips, newest first (clamped to what exists).\n    function recentTips(uint256 count) external view returns (TipEntry[] memory) {\n        uint256 len = tips.length;\n        if (count > len) count = len;\n\n        TipEntry[] memory out = new TipEntry[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            out[i] = tips[len - 1 - i];\n        }\n        return out;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current jar balance available to withdraw.\n    function balance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return token.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw the entire collected balance to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 amount = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (amount == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n\n        token.safeTransfer(owner, amount);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, amount);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1333,"cache_read_input_tokens":46381,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1333,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"8b8b847b-28e7-4cf7-b165-aa3c9174b963","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:57:08.629Z","request_id":"req_011CdMEcJoovRHy85YwPRX3J"}

MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\nimport { IERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport { SafeERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice A tip jar denominated in a single ERC20 (USDC on Base).\n * @dev Tippers must `approve` the jar for the tip amount first, then call `tip`.\n *      The jar pulls the tokens with `transferFrom`, records the tip in an\n *      on-chain feed, and lets the owner withdraw the collected balance.\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    struct TipEntry {\n        address from;\n        uint256 amount;\n        string message;\n        uint256 timestamp;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice The ERC20 accepted as tips (Base USDC).\n    IERC20 public immutable token;\n\n    /// @notice The account allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n    address public immutable owner;\n\n    /// @notice Every tip ever received, oldest first.\n    TipEntry[] public tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total tipped per address (in token base units).\n    mapping(address => uint256) public totalTippedBy;\n\n    /// @notice Lifetime total of all tips received (in token base units).\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    event Tipped(address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    error NotOwner();\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    constructor(IERC20 _token, address _owner) {\n        token = _token;\n        owner = _owner;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a tip. Caller must have approved this contract for `amount` first.\n     * @param amount Tip amount in token base units (USDC has 6 decimals, so 1 USDC = 1_000_000).\n     * @param message A short public message shown in the tip feed.\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        // Pull the tokens first so we only record tips that actually settled.\n        token.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        tips.push(TipEntry({ from: msg.sender, amount: amount, message: message, timestamp: block.timestamp }));\n        totalTippedBy[msg.sender] += amount;\n        totalTipped += amount;\n\n        emit Tipped(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips in the feed.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Return the most recent `count` tips, newest first (clamped to what exists).\n    function recentTips(uint256 count) external view returns (TipEntry[] memory) {\n        uint256 len = tips.length;\n        if (count > len) count = len;\n\n        TipEntry[] memory out = new TipEntry[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            out[i] = tips[len - 1 - i];\n        }\n        return out;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current jar balance available to withdraw.\n    function balance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return token.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw the entire collected balance to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 amount = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (amount == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n\n        token.safeTransfer(owner, amount);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, amount);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMEdfHeBkmkMN8W3Y7pc","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01AFsJ4WXa7ZjobTRWYa6LQq","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T165312Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport { IERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport { TipJar } from \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Deploy script for the TipJar contract.\n * @dev The jar is denominated in USDC. On a Base fork (or Base mainnet) this is the\n *      canonical Base USDC. Override with the USDC_ADDRESS env var if needed.\n *\n * Example:\n *   yarn deploy --file DeployTipJar.s.sol            # local anvil / Base fork\n *   yarn deploy --file DeployTipJar.s.sol --network base\n */\ncontract DeployTipJar is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    // Canonical USDC on Base (mainnet and mainnet forks share this address).\n    address constant BASE_USDC = 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913;\n\n    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n        address usdc = vm.envOr(\"USDC_ADDRESS\", BASE_USDC);\n        new TipJar(IERC20(usdc), deployer);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2029,"cache_read_input_tokens":47714,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2029,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"fca7b3ca-24e8-4565-bf3e-564da1da2106","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:57:20.985Z","request_id":"req_011CdMEddwn1LXuip32E1eft"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMEfapop42qpsRQJqH1W","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01QtyV3incC1J7a1ZcasvWw6","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T165312Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport { Test } from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport { ERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\nimport { IERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport { TipJar } from \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\n\n/// @dev 6-decimal token that mimics USDC for unit tests (no fork required).\ncontract MockUSDC is ERC20 {\n    constructor() ERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\") { }\n\n    function decimals() public pure override returns (uint8) {\n        return 6;\n    }\n\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        _mint(to, amount);\n    }\n}\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC usdc;\n    TipJar jar;\n\n    address owner = makeAddr(\"owner\");\n    address alice = makeAddr(\"alice\");\n    address bob = makeAddr(\"bob\");\n\n    uint256 constant ONE_USDC = 1e6;\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        jar = new TipJar(IERC20(address(usdc)), owner);\n        usdc.mint(alice, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address who, uint256 amount, string memory message) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_TipPullsUsdcAndRecordsFeed() public {\n        _tip(alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\");\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 95 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTippedBy(alice), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n\n        (address from, uint256 amount, string memory message,) = jar.tips(0);\n        assertEq(from, alice);\n        assertEq(amount, 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(message, \"gm\");\n    }\n\n    function test_TipEmitsEvent() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), 2 * ONE_USDC);\n        vm.expectEmit(true, false, false, true);\n        emit TipJar.Tipped(alice, 2 * ONE_USDC, \"thanks\", block.timestamp);\n        jar.tip(2 * ONE_USDC, \"thanks\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_RecentTipsNewestFirstAndClamped() public {\n        _tip(alice, 1 * ONE_USDC, \"one\");\n        _tip(bob, 2 * ONE_USDC, \"two\");\n        _tip(alice, 3 * ONE_USDC, \"three\");\n\n        TipJar.TipEntry[] memory recent = jar.recentTips(2);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 2);\n        assertEq(recent[0].message, \"three\");\n        assertEq(recent[1].message, \"two\");\n\n        // Asking for more than exists returns everything.\n        assertEq(jar.recentTips(50).length, 3);\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRevertsOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRevertsWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, \"no approval\");\n    }\n\n    function test_OwnerWithdrawsCollectedBalance() public {\n        _tip(alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"a\");\n        _tip(bob, 3 * ONE_USDC, \"b\");\n\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 8 * ONE_USDC);\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.withdraw();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), 8 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_WithdrawRevertsForNonOwner() public {\n        _tip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"a\");\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function test_WithdrawRevertsWhenEmpty() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NothingToWithdraw.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":98,"cache_read_input_tokens":52035,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":98,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"e3f46c05-52ad-433b-a7a1-ba3eeecc03a0","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:57:54.745Z","request_id":"req_011CdMEfYmHZyme7FFezk8jg"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMEhq8qYBrLTjmoJ8h9A","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the funding script. It impersonates the USDC masterMinter on the fork, configures a minter, and mints real USDC to test identities:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":338,"cache_read_input_tokens":56626,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":338,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"20f1137a-3001-426a-bda7-b7e3040df9e8","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:58:19.917Z","request_id":"req_011CdMEhotvTbqBbUYyxT1d9"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMEhq8qYBrLTjmoJ8h9A","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01SbMu5pJQrnmd8eX8PfcyGe","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T165312Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/foundry/scripts-js/fund-usdc.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n#\n# Fund test identities with real Base USDC on a local Base fork.\n#\n# The jar accepts the canonical Base USDC contract. On a fork that contract and\n# all of its balances/logic are the real thing, so we hand test accounts USDC by\n# impersonating USDC's on-chain masterMinter, registering a local minter, and\n# minting into the fork. No mainnet transaction is sent and no real money moves.\n#\n# Usage:\n#   yarn fund                         # fund the default anvil accounts (100 USDC each)\n#   yarn fund 0xYourBurnerAddress     # also fund a specific address (e.g. your browser burner)\n#   AMOUNT=250 yarn fund 0xAbc...     # fund with a custom USDC amount\n#\n# Requires a running Base fork (see `yarn fork`) reachable at $RPC_URL.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nRPC_URL=\"${RPC_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:8545}\"\nUSDC=\"${USDC_ADDRESS:-0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913}\"\nAMOUNT_USDC=\"${AMOUNT:-100}\"\n\n# Anvil account #0 — funded with ETH on any anvil chain, used here as the local USDC minter.\nMINTER=\"0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266\"\nMINTER_PK=\"0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80\"\n\n# Default recipients: anvil accounts #0, #1, #2 and the default deployer (#9).\nDEFAULT_RECIPIENTS=(\n  \"0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266\"\n  \"0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8\"\n  \"0x3C44CdDdB6a900fa2b585dd299e03d12FA4293BC\"\n  \"0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720\"\n)\nRECIPIENTS=(\"${DEFAULT_RECIPIENTS[@]}\" \"$@\")\n\n# USDC has 6 decimals.\nAMOUNT_BASE=$(cast to-unit \"$(cast to-wei \"$AMOUNT_USDC\" ether)\" gwei | cut -d. -f1)\n# Simpler/safer: AMOUNT_USDC * 1e6 via cast.\nAMOUNT_BASE=$(python3 -c \"print(int(float('$AMOUNT_USDC') * 10**6))\")\n\necho \"→ Fork RPC:        $RPC_URL\"\necho \"→ USDC:            $USDC\"\necho \"→ Amount each:     $AMOUNT_USDC USDC ($AMOUNT_BASE base units)\"\n\n# Confirm we are talking to a fork that actually has the USDC contract deployed.\nif [ \"$(cast code \"$USDC\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\")\" = \"0x\" ]; then\n  echo \"✗ No contract at $USDC on $RPC_URL — is your Base fork running? (yarn fork)\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\nMASTER_MINTER=$(cast call \"$USDC\" \"masterMinter()(address)\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\")\necho \"→ masterMinter:    $MASTER_MINTER\"\n\n# Impersonate the masterMinter and give it gas money on the fork.\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount \"$MASTER_MINTER\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" >/dev/null\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance \"$MASTER_MINTER\" 0xDE0B6B3A7640000 --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" >/dev/null # 1 ETH\n\n# Authorize our local minter with a generous allowance.\ncast send \"$USDC\" \"configureMinter(address,uint256)\" \"$MINTER\" 1000000000000000 \\\n  --from \"$MASTER_MINTER\" --unlocked --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" >/dev/null\n\ncast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount \"$MASTER_MINTER\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" >/dev/null\n\n# Mint to each recipient from the (real, signed) minter account.\nfor addr in \"${RECIPIENTS[@]}\"; do\n  cast send \"$USDC\" \"mint(address,uint256)\" \"$addr\" \"$AMOUNT_BASE\" \\\n    --private-key \"$MINTER_PK\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" >/dev/null\n  bal=$(cast call \"$USDC\" \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" \"$addr\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" | cut -d' ' -f1)\n  printf \"  ✓ %s  →  %s USDC\\n\" \"$addr\" \"$(python3 -c \"print(int('$bal')/10**6)\")\"\ndone\n\necho \"✓ Done. Connect one of these accounts in the app and send a tip.\"\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":338,"cache_read_input_tokens":56626,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":338,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"a664513b-65a8-4ca3-b024-b566d5694910","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:58:45.499Z","request_id":"req_011CdMEhotvTbqBbUYyxT1d9"}

On a fork that contract and\n# all of its balances/logic are the real thing, so we hand test accounts USDC by\n# impersonating USDC's on-chain masterMinter, registering a local minter, and\n# minting into the fork. No mainnet transaction is sent and no real money moves.\n#\n# Usage:\n#   yarn fund                         # fund the default anvil accounts (100 USDC each)\n#   yarn fund 0xYourBurnerAddress     # also fund a specific address (e.g. your browser burner)\n#   AMOUNT=250 yarn fund 0xAbc...     # fund with a custom USDC amount\n#\n# Requires a running Base fork (see `yarn fork`) reachable at $RPC_URL.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nRPC_URL=\"${RPC_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:8545}\"\nUSDC=\"${USDC_ADDRESS:-0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913}\"\nAMOUNT_USDC=\"${AMOUNT:-100}\"\n\n# Anvil account #0 — funded with ETH on any anvil chain, used here as the local USDC minter.\nMINTER=\"0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266\"\nMINTER_PK=\"0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80\"\n\n# Default recipients: anvil accounts #0, #1, #2 and the default deployer (#9).\nDEFAULT_RECIPIENTS=(\n  \"0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266\"\n  \"0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8\"\n  \"0x3C44CdDdB6a900fa2b585dd299e03d12FA4293BC\"\n  \"0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720\"\n)\nRECIPIENTS=(\"${DEFAULT_RECIPIENTS[@]}\" \"$@\")\n\n# USDC has 6 decimals.\nAMOUNT_BASE=$(cast to-unit \"$(cast to-wei \"$AMOUNT_USDC\" ether)\" gwei | cut -d. -f1)\n# Simpler/safer: AMOUNT_USDC * 1e6 via cast.\nAMOUNT_BASE=$(python3 -c \"print(int(float('$AMOUNT_USDC') * 10**6))\")\n\necho \"→ Fork RPC:        $RPC_URL\"\necho \"→ USDC:            $USDC\"\necho \"→ Amount each:     $AMOUNT_USDC USDC ($AMOUNT_BASE base units)\"\n\n# Confirm we are talking to a fork that actually has the USDC contract deployed.\nif [ \"$(cast code \"$USDC\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\")\" = \"0x\" ]; then\n  echo \"✗ No contract at $USDC on $RPC_URL — is your Base fork running? (yarn fork)\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\nMASTER_MINTER=$(cast call \"$USDC\" \"masterMinter()(address)\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\")\necho \"→ masterMinter:    $MASTER_MINTER\"\n\n# Impersonate the masterMinter and give it gas money on the fork.\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount \"$MASTER_MINTER\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" >/dev/null\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance \"$MASTER_MINTER\" 0xDE0B6B3A7640000 --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" >/dev/null # 1 ETH\n\n# Authorize our local minter with a generous allowance.\ncast send \"$USDC\" \"configureMinter(address,uint256)\" \"$MINTER\" 1000000000000000 \\\n  --from \"$MASTER_MINTER\" --unlocked --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" >/dev/null\n\ncast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount \"$MASTER_MINTER\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" >/dev/null\n\n# Mint to each recipient from the (real, signed) minter account.\nfor addr in \"${RECIPIENTS[@]}\"; do\n  cast send \"$USDC\" \"mint(address,uint256)\" \"$addr\" \"$AMOUNT_BASE\" \\\n    --private-key \"$MINTER_PK\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" >/dev/null\n  bal=$(cast call \"$USDC\" \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" \"$addr\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" | cut -d' ' -f1)\n  printf \"  ✓ %s  →  %s USDC\\n\" \"$addr\" \"$(python3 -c \"print(int('$bal')/10**6)\")\"\ndone\n\necho \"✓ Done. Connect one of these accounts in the app and send a tip.\"\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

On a fork that contract and\n# all of its balances/logic are the real thing, so we hand test accounts USDC by\n# impersonating USDC's on-chain masterMinter, registering a local minter, and\n# minting into the fork. No mainnet transaction is sent and no real money moves.\n#\n# Usage:\n#   yarn fund                         # fund the default anvil accounts (100 USDC each)\n#   yarn fund 0xYourBurnerAddress     # also fund a specific address (e.g. your browser burner)\n#   AMOUNT=250 yarn fund 0xAbc...     # fund with a custom USDC amount\n#\n# Requires a running Base fork (see `yarn fork`) reachable at $RPC_URL.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nRPC_URL=\"${RPC_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:8545}\"\nUSDC=\"${USDC_ADDRESS:-0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913}\"\nAMOUNT_USDC=\"${AMOUNT:-100}\"\n\n# Anvil account #0 — funded with ETH on any anvil chain, used here as the local USDC minter.\nMINTER=\"0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266\"\nMINTER_PK=\"0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80\"\n\n# Default recipients: anvil accounts #0, #1, #2 and the default deployer (#9).\nDEFAULT_RECIPIENTS=(\n  \"0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266\"\n  \"0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8\"\n  \"0x3C44CdDdB6a900fa2b585dd299e03d12FA4293BC\"\n  \"0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720\"\n)\nRECIPIENTS=(\"${DEFAULT_RECIPIENTS[@]}\" \"$@\")\n\n# USDC has 6 decimals.\nAMOUNT_BASE=$(awk \"BEGIN { printf \\\"%d\\\", $AMOUNT_USDC * 1000000 }\")\n\necho \"→ Fork RPC:        $RPC_URL\"\necho \"→ USDC:            $USDC\"\necho \"→ Amount each:     $AMOUNT_USDC USDC ($AMOUNT_BASE base units)\"\n\n# Confirm we are talking to a fork that actually has the USDC contract deployed.\nif [ \"$(cast code \"$USDC\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\")\" = \"0x\" ]; then\n  echo \"✗ No contract at $USDC on $RPC_URL — is your Base fork running? (yarn fork)\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\nMASTER_MINTER=$(cast call \"$USDC\" \"masterMinter()(address)\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\")\necho \"→ masterMinter:    $MASTER_MINTER\"\n\n# Impersonate the masterMinter and give it gas money on the fork.\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount \"$MASTER_MINTER\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" >/dev/null\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance \"$MASTER_MINTER\" 0xDE0B6B3A7640000 --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" >/dev/null # 1 ETH\n\n# Authorize our local minter with a generous allowance.\ncast send \"$USDC\" \"configureMinter(address,uint256)\" \"$MINTER\" 1000000000000000 \\\n  --from \"$MASTER_MINTER\" --unlocked --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" >/dev/null\n\ncast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount \"$MASTER_MINTER\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" >/dev/null\n\n# Mint to each recipient from the (real, signed) minter account.\nfor addr in \"${RECIPIENTS[@]}\"; do\n  cast send \"$USDC\" \"mint(address,uint256)\" \"$addr\" \"$AMOUNT_BASE\" \\\n    --private-key \"$MINTER_PK\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" >/dev/null\n  bal=$(cast call \"$USDC\" \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" \"$addr\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" | cut -d' ' -f1)\n  printf \"  ✓ %s  →  %s USDC\\n\" \"$addr\" \"$(python3 -c \"print(int('$bal')/10**6)\")\"\ndone\n\necho \"✓ Done. Connect one of these accounts in the app and send a tip.\"\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":63,"oldLines":7,"newStart":63,"newLines":7,"lines":["   cast send \"$USDC\" \"mint(address,uint256)\" \"$addr\" \"$AMOUNT_BASE\" \\","     --private-key \"$MINTER_PK\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" >/dev/null","   bal=$(cast call \"$USDC\" \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" \"$addr\" --rpc-url \"$RPC_URL\" | cut -d' ' -f1)","-  printf \"  ✓ %s  →  %s USDC\\n\" \"$addr\" \"$(python3 -c \"print(int('$bal')/10**6)\")\"","+  printf \"  ✓ %s  →  %s USDC\\n\" \"$addr\" \"$(awk \"BEGIN { printf \\\"%.2f\\\", $bal / 1000000 }\")\""," done"," "," echo \"✓ Done. Connect one of these accounts in the app and send a tip.\""]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

-f \"$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)\" ]; then \t\techo \"Error: Deploy script '$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)' not found\"; \t\texit 1; \tfi\n\t@if [ \"$(RPC_URL)\" = \"localhost\" ]; then \t\tif [ \"$(ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT)\" = \"scaffold-eth-default\" ]; then \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --password localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\telse \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\tfi \telse \t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --ffi; \tfi\n\n# Deploy and generate ABIs\ndeploy-and-generate-abis: deploy generate-abis \n\n# Generate TypeScript ABIs\ngenerate-abis:\n\tnode scripts-js/generateTsAbis.js\n\n# List account\naccount:\n\t@node scripts-js/checkAccountBalance.js\n\n# Get address of a keystore\nget-address:\n\t@cast wallet address --account $(ACCOUNT_NAME)\n\n# Compile contracts\ncompile:\n\tforge compile\n\n# Flatten contracts\nflatten:\n\tforge flatten\n\n# Format code\nformat:\n\tforge fmt && prettier --write ./scripts-js/**/*.js\n\n# Lint code\nlint:\n\tforge fmt --check && prettier --check ./scripts-js/**/*.js\n\n# Run tests\ntest:\n\tforge test\n\n# Verify contracts\nverify:\n\tforge script script/VerifyAll.s.sol --ffi --rpc-url $(RPC_URL)\n\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":5,"newStart":1,"newLines":7,"lines":["-.PHONY: build deploy generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork format lint test verify","+.PHONY: build deploy generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork fork-base fund format lint test verify"," ","+BASE_RPC_URL ?= https://mainnet.base.org","+"," DEPLOY_SCRIPT ?= script/Deploy.s.sol"," "," # setup wallet for anvil"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMEwVFe8j9q1coYQLKTq","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GJEEwNVioiSZXLqaQ4J8RV","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T165312Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/TipForm.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\nexport const TipForm = () => {\n  const { address } = useAccount();\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);\n\n  const { data: tipJar } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [address],\n  });\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [address, tipJar?.address],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeTipJar } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const formattedBalance =\n    usdcBalance === undefined ? undefined : Number(formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS)).toLocaleString();\n\n  const handleTip = async () => {\n    if (!address) {\n      notification.error(\"Connect a wallet first\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!tipJar?.address) {\n      notification.error(\"TipJar contract not found on this network. Deploy it first.\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    let parsed: bigint;\n    try {\n      parsed = parseUnits(amount.trim(), USDC_DECIMALS);\n    } catch {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid USDC amount\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (parsed <= 0n) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter an amount greater than zero\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (usdcBalance !== undefined && parsed > usdcBalance) {\n      notification.error(\"Amount exceeds your USDC balance\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      setBusy(true);\n      // USDC is an ERC20: approve the jar for the tip amount, then let it pull the tokens.\n      if (allowance === undefined || allowance < parsed) {\n        await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [tipJar.address, parsed] });\n      }\n      await writeTipJar({ functionName: \"tip\", args: [parsed, message.trim()] });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      // useScaffoldWriteContract already surfaces a toast; nothing else to do here.\n      console.error(e);\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send a tip</h2>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control w-full\">\n          <div className=\"label\">\n            <span className=\"label-text\">Amount (USDC)</span>\n            {formattedBalance !== undefined && (\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                className=\"label-text-alt link\"\n                onClick={() => usdcBalance && setAmount(formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS))}\n              >\n                Balance: {formattedBalance} — Max\n              </button>\n            )}\n          </div>\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            step=\"0.01\"\n            placeholder=\"5.00\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control w-full\">\n          <div className=\"label\">\n            <span className=\"label-text\">Message</span>\n            <span className=\"label-text-alt\">{message.length}/140</span>\n          </div>\n          <input\n            type=\"text\"\n            maxLength={140}\n            placeholder=\"Thanks for building this!\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full\"\n            value={message}\n            onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" onClick={handleTip} disabled={busy || !address}>\n          {busy ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : null}\n          {busy ? \"Confirming…\" : \"Send tip\"}\n        </button>\n        <p className=\"text-xs text-base-content/60\">\n          The first tip needs two wallet confirmations: one to approve USDC, one to send the tip.\n        </p>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":985,"cache_read_input_tokens":74962,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":985,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":64,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"6cec4401-0ace-43f4-b6be-8714d2738527","timestamp":"2026-07-24T17:01:28.659Z","request_id":"req_011CdMEwU4D2sg9GWczD6WpP"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016t54gqVWRWj78XfsEBZAnW","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T165312Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/foundry/Makefile has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"c107900c-322a-4c64-8a2e-06af633bc330","timestamp":"2026-07-24T17:05:31.764Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T165312Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/foundry/Makefile","oldString":".PHONY: build deploy generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork fork-base fund format lint test verify\n\nBASE_RPC_URL ?= https://mainnet.base.org","newString":".PHONY: build deploy deploy-fork generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork fork-base fund format lint test verify\n\nBASE_RPC_URL ?= https://mainnet.base.org","originalFile":".PHONY: build deploy generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork fork-base fund format lint test verify\n\nBASE_RPC_URL ?= https://mainnet.base.org\n\nDEPLOY_SCRIPT ?= script/Deploy.s.sol\n\n# setup wallet for anvil\nsetup-anvil-wallet:\n\tshx rm ~/.foundry/keystores/scaffold-eth-default 2>/dev/null; \tshx rm -rf broadcast/Deploy.s.sol/31337\n\tcast wallet import --private-key 0x2a871d0798f97d79848a013d4936a73bf4cc922c825d33c1cf7073dff6d409c6 --unsafe-password 'localhost' scaffold-eth-default\n\n# Start local chain\nchain: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil\n\n# Start a fork\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337\n\n# Start a Base mainnet fork (real Base state, local chain id 31337, no real money at risk)\nfork-base: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url $(BASE_RPC_URL) --chain-id 31337\n\n# Mint real USDC to test identities on the running Base fork\nfund:\n\t./scripts-js/fund-usdc.sh $(ADDRESSES)\n\n# Deploy the contracts\ndeploy:\n\t@if [ ! -f \"$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)\" ]; then \t\techo \"Error: Deploy script '$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)' not found\"; \t\texit 1; \tfi\n\t@if [ \"$(RPC_URL)\" = \"localhost\" ]; then \t\tif [ \"$(ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT)\" = \"scaffold-eth-default\" ]; then \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --password localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\telse \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\tfi \telse \t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --ffi; \tfi\n\n# Deploy and generate ABIs\ndeploy-and-generate-abis: deploy generate-abis \n\n# Generate TypeScript ABIs\ngenerate-abis:\n\tnode scripts-js/generateTsAbis.js\n\n# List account\naccount:\n\t@node scripts-js/checkAccountBalance.js\n\n# Get address of a keystore\nget-address:\n\t@cast wallet address --account $(ACCOUNT_NAME)\n\n# Compile contracts\ncompile:\n\tforge compile\n\n# Flatten contracts\nflatten:\n\tforge flatten\n\n# Format code\nformat:\n\tforge fmt && prettier --write ./scripts-js/**/*.js\n\n# Lint code\nlint:\n\tforge fmt --check && prettier --check ./scripts-js/**/*.js\n\n# Run tests\ntest:\n\tforge test\n\n# Verify contracts\nverify:\n\tforge script script/VerifyAll.s.sol --ffi --rpc-url $(RPC_URL)\n\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":4,"newStart":1,"newLines":4,"lines":["-.PHONY: build deploy generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork fork-base fund format lint test verify","+.PHONY: build deploy deploy-fork generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork fork-base fund format lint test verify"," "," BASE_RPC_URL ?= https://mainnet.base.org"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMFGssxDj168Apg7VNTU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01TDJ8RERZFVj5WrwNQq5ouu","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T165312Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/foundry/Makefile","old_string":"# Mint real USDC to test identities on the running Base fork\nfund:\n\t./scripts-js/fund-usdc.sh $(ADDRESSES)","new_string":"# Mint real USDC to test identities on the running Base fork\nfund:\n\t./scripts-js/fund-usdc.sh $(ADDRESSES)\n\n# Deploy to the local Base fork and regenerate the frontend ABIs.\n# Passes an explicit --sender because on a fork the default account already has a\n# nonce history on Base; without it forge derives the wrong nonce (\"nonce too low\").\nDEPLOY_SCRIPT ?= script/Deploy.s.sol\ndeploy-fork:\n\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --account scaffold-eth-default \\\n\t\t--sender $$(cast wallet address --account scaffold-eth-default --password localhost) \\\n\t\t--password localhost --broadcast --ffi --slow\n\t$(MAKE) generate-abis"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1785,"cache_read_input_tokens":94190,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1785,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":55,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"b6fb8a13-4667-4bcf-8a15-ad55490b3368","timestamp":"2026-07-24T17:05:40.994Z","request_id":"req_011CdMFGjJX9C4bZKKMHTTM4"}

without it forge derives the wrong nonce (\"nonce too low\").\nDEPLOY_SCRIPT ?= script/Deploy.s.sol\ndeploy-fork:","newString":"# nonce history on Base; without it forge derives the wrong nonce (\"nonce too low\").\ndeploy-fork:","originalFile":".PHONY: build deploy deploy-fork generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork fork-base fund format lint test verify\n\nBASE_RPC_URL ?= https://mainnet.base.org\n\nDEPLOY_SCRIPT ?= script/Deploy.s.sol\n\n# setup wallet for anvil\nsetup-anvil-wallet:\n\tshx rm ~/.foundry/keystores/scaffold-eth-default 2>/dev/null; \tshx rm -rf broadcast/Deploy.s.sol/31337\n\tcast wallet import --private-key 0x2a871d0798f97d79848a013d4936a73bf4cc922c825d33c1cf7073dff6d409c6 --unsafe-password 'localhost' scaffold-eth-default\n\n# Start local chain\nchain: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil\n\n# Start a fork\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337\n\n# Start a Base mainnet fork (real Base state, local chain id 31337, no real money at risk)\nfork-base: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url $(BASE_RPC_URL) --chain-id 31337\n\n# Mint real USDC to test identities on the running Base fork\nfund:\n\t./scripts-js/fund-usdc.sh $(ADDRESSES)\n\n# Deploy to the local Base fork and regenerate the frontend ABIs.\n# Passes an explicit --sender because on a fork the default account already has a\n# nonce history on Base; without it forge derives the wrong nonce (\"nonce too low\").\nDEPLOY_SCRIPT ?= script/Deploy.s.sol\ndeploy-fork:\n\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --account scaffold-eth-default \\\n\t\t--sender $$(cast wallet address --account scaffold-eth-default --password localhost) \\\n\t\t--password localhost --broadcast --ffi --slow\n\t$(MAKE) generate-abis\n\n# Deploy the contracts\ndeploy:\n\t@if [ ! -f \"$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)\" ]; then \t\techo \"Error: Deploy script '$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)' not found\"; \t\texit 1; \tfi\n\t@if [ \"$(RPC_URL)\" = \"localhost\" ]; then \t\tif [ \"$(ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT)\" = \"scaffold-eth-default\" ]; then \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --password localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\telse \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\tfi \telse \t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --ffi; \tfi\n\n# Deploy and generate ABIs\ndeploy-and-generate-abis: deploy generate-abis \n\n# Generate TypeScript ABIs\ngenerate-abis:\n\tnode scripts-js/generateTsAbis.js\n\n# List account\naccount:\n\t@node scripts-js/checkAccountBalance.js\n\n# Get address of a keystore\nget-address:\n\t@cast wallet address --account $(ACCOUNT_NAME)\n\n# Compile contracts\ncompile:\n\tforge compile\n\n# Flatten contracts\nflatten:\n\tforge flatten\n\n# Format code\nformat:\n\tforge fmt && prettier --write ./scripts-js/**/*.js\n\n# Lint code\nlint:\n\tforge fmt --check && prettier --check ./scripts-js/**/*.js\n\n# Run tests\ntest:\n\tforge test\n\n# Verify contracts\nverify:\n\tforge script script/VerifyAll.s.sol --ffi --rpc-url $(RPC_URL)\n\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":28,"oldLines":7,"newStart":28,"newLines":6,"lines":[" # Deploy to the local Base fork and regenerate the frontend ABIs."," # Passes an explicit --sender because on a fork the default account already has a"," # nonce history on Base;    walletClient = data;\n  }\n\n  const result: TransactionFunc = async (tx, options) => {\n    if (!walletClient) {\n      notification.error(\"Cannot access account\");\n      console.error(\"⚡️ ~ file: useTransactor.tsx ~ error\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    let notificationId = null;\n    let transactionHash: Hash | undefined = undefined;\n    let transactionReceipt: TransactionReceipt | undefined;\n    let blockExplorerTxURL = \"\";\n    let chainId: number = scaffoldConfig.targetNetworks[0].id;\n    try {\n      chainId = await walletClient.getChainId();\n      // Get full transaction from public client\n      const publicClient = getPublicClient(wagmiConfig);\n\n      notificationId = notification.loading(<TxnNotification message=\"Awaiting for user confirmation\" />);\n      if (typeof tx === \"function\") {\n        // Tx is already prepared by the caller\n        const result = await tx();\n        transactionHash = result;\n      } else if (tx != null) {\n        transactionHash = await walletClient.sendTransaction(tx as SendTransactionParameters);\n      } else {\n        throw new Error(\"Incorrect transaction passed to transactor\");\n      }\n      notification.remove(notificationId);\n\n      blockExplorerTxURL = chainId ? getBlockExplorerTxLink(chainId, transactionHash) : \"\";\n\n      notificationId = notification.loading(\n        <TxnNotification message=\"Waiting for transaction to complete.\" blockExplorerLink={blockExplorerTxURL} />,\n      );\n\n      transactionReceipt = await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({\n        hash: transactionHash,\n        confirmations: options?.blockConfirmations,\n      });\n      notification.remove(notificationId);\n\n      if (transactionReceipt.status === \"reverted\") throw new Error(\"Transaction reverted\");\n\n      notification.success(\n        <TxnNotification message=\"Transaction completed successfully!\" blockExplorerLink={blockExplorerTxURL} />,\n        {\n          icon: \"🎉\",\n        },\n      );\n\n      if (options?.onBlockConfirmation) options.onBlockConfirmation(transactionReceipt);\n    } catch (error: any) (\n        <a href={blockExplorerLink} target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer\" className=\"block link\">\n          check out transaction\n        </a>\n      ) : null}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\n/**\n * Runs Transaction passed in to returned function showing UI feedback.\n * @param _walletClient - Optional wallet client to use. If not provided, will use the one from useWalletClient.\n * @returns function that takes in transaction function as callback, shows UI feedback for transaction and returns a promise of the transaction hash\n */\nexport const useTransactor = (_walletClient?: WalletClient): TransactionFunc => {\n  let walletClient = _walletClient;\n  const { data } = useWalletClient();\n  if (walletClient === undefined && data) {\n    walletClient = data;\n  }\n\n  const result: TransactionFunc = async (tx, options) => {\n    if (!walletClient) {\n      notification.error(\"Cannot access account\");\n      console.error(\"⚡️ ~ file: useTransactor.tsx ~ error\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    let notificationId = null;\n    let transactionHash: Hash | undefined = undefined;\n    let transactionReceipt: TransactionReceipt | undefined;\n    let blockExplorerTxURL = \"\";\n    let chainId: number = scaffoldConfig.targetNetworks[0].id;\n    try {\n      chainId = await walletClient.getChainId();\n      // Get full transaction from public client\n      const publicClient = getPublicClient(wagmiConfig);\n\n      notificationId = notification.loading(<TxnNotification message=\"Awaiting for user confirmation\" />);\n      if (typeof tx === \"function\") {\n        // Tx is already prepared by the caller\n        const result = await tx();\n        transactionHash = result;\n      } else if (tx != null) {\n        transactionHash = await walletClient.sendTransaction(tx as SendTransactionParameters);\n      } else {\n        throw new Error(\"Incorrect transaction passed to transactor\");\n      }\n      notification.remove(notificationId);\n\n      blockExplorerTxURL = chainId ? getBlockExplorerTxLink(chainId, transactionHash) : \"\";\n\n      notificationId = notification.loading(\n        <TxnNotification message=\"Waiting for transaction to complete.\" blockExplorerLink={blockExplorerTxURL} />,\n      );\n\n      transactionReceipt = await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({\n        hash: transactionHash,\n        confirmations: options?.blockConfirmations,\n      });\n      notification.remove(notificationId);\n\n      if (transactionReceipt.status === \"reverted\") throw new Error(\"Transaction reverted\");\n\n      notification.success(\n        <TxnNotification message=\"Transaction completed successfully!\" blockExplorerLink={blockExplorerTxURL} />,\n        {\n          icon: \"🎉\",\n        },\n      );\n\n      if (options?.onBlockConfirmation) options.onBlockConfirmation(transactionReceipt);\n    } catch (error: any) {\n      if (notificationId) {\n        notification.remove(notificationId);\n      }\n      console.error(\"⚡️ ~ file: useTransactor.ts ~ error\", error);\n      const message = getParsedErrorWithAllAbis(error, chainId as AllowedChainIds);\n\n      // if receipt was reverted, show notification with block explorer link and return error\n      if (transactionReceipt?.status === \"reverted\") {\n        notification.error(<TxnNotification message={message} blockExplorerLink={blockExplorerTxURL} />);\n        throw error;\n      }\n\n      notification.error(message);\n      throw error;\n    }\n\n    return transactionHash;\n  };\n\n  return result;\n};\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":56,"oldLines":6,"newStart":56,"newLines":9,"lines":["       chainId = await walletClient.getChainId();","       // Get full transaction from public client","       const publicClient = getPublicClient(wagmiConfig);","+      if (!publicClient) {","+        throw new Error(\"Cannot access public client\");","+      }"," ","       notificationId = notification.loading(<TxnNotification message=\"Awaiting for user confirmation\" />);","       if (typeof tx === \"function\") {"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

But only once.\n// Annotated as a non-empty tuple so wagmi's `chains` type accepts it regardless of which\n// single target network scaffold.config resolves to at build time.\nexport const enabledChains: readonly [Chain, ...Chain[]] = targetNetworks.find(\n  (network: Chain) => network.id === 1,\n)\n  ? targetNetworks\n  : [...targetNetworks, mainnet];","originalFile":"import { wagmiConnectors } from \"./wagmiConnectors\";\nimport { Chain, createClient, fallback, http } from \"viem\";\nimport { hardhat, mainnet } from \"viem/chains\";\nimport { createConfig } from \"wagmi\";\nimport scaffoldConfig, { DEFAULT_ALCHEMY_API_KEY, ScaffoldConfig } from \"~~/scaffold.config\";\nimport { getAlchemyHttpUrl } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\n\nconst { targetNetworks } = scaffoldConfig;\n\n// We always want to have mainnet enabled (ENS resolution, ETH price, etc). {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01K62MNYCpv8KcX1PWPkEXA5","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n2\t\n3\t<h4 align=\"center\">\n4\t  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n5\t  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n6\t</h4>\n7\t\n8\t🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n9\t\n10\t> [!NOTE]\n11\t> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n12\t\n13\t⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n14\t\n15\t- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n16\t- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n17\t- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n18\t- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n19\t- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n20\t\n21\t![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n22\t\n23\t## Requirements\n24\t\n25\tBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n26\t\n27\t- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n28\t- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n29\t- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n30\t\n31\t## Quickstart\n32\t\n33\tTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n34\t\n35\t1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n36\t\n37\t```\n38\tcd my-dapp-example\n39\tyarn install\n40\t```\n41\t\n42\t2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n43\t\n44\t```\n45\tyarn chain\n46\t```\n47\t\n48\tThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n49\t\n50\t3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n51\t\n52\t```\n53\tyarn deploy\n54\t```\n55\t\n56\tThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n57\t\n58\t4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n59\t\n60\t```\n61\tyarn start\n62\t```\n63\t\n64\tVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n65\t\n66\tRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n67\t\n68\t- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n69\t- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n70\t- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n71\t\n72\t\n73\t## Documentation\n74\t\n75\tVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n76\t\n77\tTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n78\t\n79\t## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n80\t\n81\tWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n82\t\n83\tPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"ebda167d-d2c4-418d-b91c-7ec8bfb7924b","timestamp":"2026-07-24T17:13:10.672Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T165312Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/README.md","content":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n\n```\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n\n4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n\n```\nyarn start\n```\n\nVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\nRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n\n- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","numLines":83,"startLine":1,"totalLines":83}}}
