First, README with the exact local workflow:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3297,"cache_read_input_tokens":129458,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3297,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"f7aa5b73-4cde-4748-87e9-57b96aa27762","timestamp":"2026-07-24T17:13:16.536Z","request_id":"req_011CdMFraa72k7JdjHxRRC4c"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMFrgYmX67cHNYNSYYv2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01RfMyikkhwZ4qDFkxfy9Zaw","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T165312Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/README.md","content":"# 💸 USDC Tip Jar on Base\n\nA [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io) (Foundry) dApp that accepts **USDC tips on Base**.\nIt ships two things:\n\n- **`TipJar` contract** — pulls USDC via `approve` + `transferFrom`, records every tip\n  (sender, amount, message, timestamp) in an on-chain feed, and lets the owner withdraw.\n- **Tip page** — jar stats, a live tip feed, and a form to approve + send a tip.\n\nBase USDC: [`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`](https://basescan.org/address/0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913)\n\nThe team develops and demos **locally against real Base state**: a Base mainnet fork served\non a local anvil chain. Tips move the **real USDC contract's** balances between test\nidentities, but nothing touches mainnet and **no real money is at risk**.\n\n> Shipping the site? See **[DEPLOY.md](./DEPLOY.md)** for the static IPFS build, upload, and\n> post-deploy verification commands.\n\n---\n\n## Requirements\n\n- [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation) (`forge`, `anvil`, `cast`)\n- **Node 20 or 22** (the static IPFS export crashes on Node ≥ 23) and Yarn v3+\n- `git`\n\nInstall dependencies once:\n\n```bash\nyarn install\n```\n\n---\n\n## Local workflow (real Base state, no real money)\n\nRun each numbered step in its own terminal. Everything targets a Base **fork** at\n`http://127.0.0.1:8545` with chain id `31337`.\n\n### 1. Start a Base mainnet fork\n\n```bash\nyarn fork:base\n```\n\nThis runs `anvil --fork-url https://mainnet.base.org --chain-id 31337`. The fork has the\nreal Base USDC contract and all of its balances/logic — it just runs on your machine.\n\n> Prefer your own RPC (the public endpoint is rate-limited)? Pass one in:\n> `yarn workspace @se-2/foundry fork:base` with `BASE_RPC_URL` set, e.g.\n> `BASE_RPC_URL=https://base-mainnet.g.alchemy.com/v2/<key> yarn fork:base`.\n\n### 2. Deploy the TipJar to the fork\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy:fork\n```\n\nDeploys `TipJar(USDC, deployer)` and regenerates the frontend ABIs into\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`.\n\n> Why `deploy:fork` instead of `yarn deploy`? On a Base fork the default deployer account\n> already has a transaction history on real Base, so `forge` needs an explicit `--sender`\n> to read the right nonce. `yarn deploy:fork` passes it for you. (`yarn deploy` is still the\n> command for a fresh anvil chain or a live network.)\n\n### 3. Fund test identities with real USDC\n\n```bash\n# Fund the default anvil accounts (100 USDC each)\nyarn fund\n\n# Also fund a specific address — e.g. copy your in-app burner wallet address:\nyarn fund 0xYourBurnerAddress\n\n# Custom amount:\nAMOUNT=250 yarn fund 0xYourBurnerAddress\n```\n\n`yarn fund` impersonates USDC's on-chain `masterMinter` on the fork, registers a local\nminter, and mints real USDC to each recipient. No mainnet transaction is sent.\n\n### 4. Start the frontend\n\n```bash\nyarn start\n```\n\nOpen **http://localhost:3000**.\n\n### 5. Demo a tip\n\n1. Connect the **Burner Wallet** (shown automatically on the local network). Copy its\n   address and fund it: `yarn fund <burnerAddress>`.\n2. Enter an amount and a message, then **Send tip**. The first tip asks for two wallet\n   confirmations: one to `approve` USDC, one to `tip`.\n3. The tip appears in the feed and the jar stats update — real USDC just moved from your\n   test identity into the jar.\n4. To withdraw, connect the **owner** account (the deployer,\n   `0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720`) and call `withdraw()` from the\n   `Debug Contracts` page.\n\n---\n\n## Contract tests\n\n```bash\nyarn foundry:test\n```\n\nUnit tests use a 6-decimal mock USDC (no fork needed) and cover the tip/approve flow, the\nfeed ordering, event emission, access control, and withdrawal.\n\n---\n\n## Project layout\n\n| Path | What |\n| --- | --- |\n| `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol` | The tip jar contract |\n| `packages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol` | Deploy script (uses Base USDC, override with `USDC_ADDRESS`) |\n| `packages/foundry/scripts-js/fund-usdc.sh` | Mints real USDC to test identities on the fork |\n| `packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol` | Contract tests |\n| `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` | Tip page (stats + feed + form) |\n| `packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/` | `JarStats`, `TipFeed`, `TipForm` |\n| `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts` | USDC ABI (+ Base TipJar for production) |\n| `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` | Targets Base fork locally, Base mainnet for the IPFS build |\n\n---\n\nBuilt with [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3297,"cache_read_input_tokens":129458,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3297,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"5f14a3cf-816e-4d9f-b466-774cdcb6e273","timestamp":"2026-07-24T17:13:46.651Z","request_id":"req_011CdMFraa72k7JdjHxRRC4c"}

Tips move the **real USDC contract's** balances between test\nidentities, but nothing touches mainnet and **no real money is at risk**.\n\n> Shipping the site? See **[DEPLOY.md](./DEPLOY.md)** for the static IPFS build, upload, and\n> post-deploy verification commands.\n\n---\n\n## Requirements\n\n- [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation) (`forge`, `anvil`, `cast`)\n- **Node 20 or 22** (the static IPFS export crashes on Node ≥ 23) and Yarn v3+\n- `git`\n\nInstall dependencies once:\n\n```bash\nyarn install\n```\n\n---\n\n## Local workflow (real Base state, no real money)\n\nRun each numbered step in its own terminal. Everything targets a Base **fork** at\n`http://127.0.0.1:8545` with chain id `31337`.\n\n### 1. Start a Base mainnet fork\n\n```bash\nyarn fork:base\n```\n\nThis runs `anvil --fork-url https://mainnet.base.org --chain-id 31337`. The fork has the\nreal Base USDC contract and all of its balances/logic — it just runs on your machine.\n\n> Prefer your own RPC (the public endpoint is rate-limited)? Pass one in:\n> `yarn workspace @se-2/foundry fork:base` with `BASE_RPC_URL` set, e.g.\n> `BASE_RPC_URL=https://base-mainnet.g.alchemy.com/v2/<key> yarn fork:base`.\n\n### 2. Deploy the TipJar to the fork\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy:fork\n```\n\nDeploys `TipJar(USDC, deployer)` and regenerates the frontend ABIs into\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`.\n\n> Why `deploy:fork` instead of `yarn deploy`? On a Base fork the default deployer account\n> already has a transaction history on real Base, so `forge` needs an explicit `--sender`\n> to read the right nonce. `yarn deploy:fork` passes it for you. (`yarn deploy` is still the\n> command for a fresh anvil chain or a live network.)\n\n### 3. Fund test identities with real USDC\n\n```bash\n# Fund the default anvil accounts (100 USDC each)\nyarn fund\n\n# Also fund a specific address — e.g. copy your in-app burner wallet address:\nyarn fund 0xYourBurnerAddress\n\n# Custom amount:\nAMOUNT=250 yarn fund 0xYourBurnerAddress\n```\n\n`yarn fund` impersonates USDC's on-chain `masterMinter` on the fork, registers a local\nminter, and mints real USDC to each recipient. No mainnet transaction is sent.\n\n### 4. Start the frontend\n\n```bash\nyarn start\n```\n\nOpen **http://localhost:3000**.\n\n### 5. Demo a tip\n\n1. Connect the **Burner Wallet** (shown automatically on the local network). Copy its\n   address and fund it: `yarn fund <burnerAddress>`.\n2. Enter an amount and a message, then **Send tip**. The first tip asks for two wallet\n   confirmations: one to `approve` USDC, one to `tip`.\n3. The tip appears in the feed and the jar stats update — real USDC just moved from your\n   test identity into the jar.\n4. To withdraw, connect the **owner** account (the deployer,\n   `0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720`) and call `withdraw()` from the\n   `Debug Contracts` page.\n\n---\n\n## Contract tests\n\n```bash\nyarn foundry:test\n```\n\nUnit tests use a 6-decimal mock USDC (no fork needed) and cover the tip/approve flow, the\nfeed ordering, event emission, access control, and withdrawal.\n\n---\n\n## Project layout\n\n| Path | What |\n| --- | --- |\n| `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol` | The tip jar contract |\n| `packages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol` | Deploy script (uses Base USDC, override with `USDC_ADDRESS`) |\n| `packages/foundry/scripts-js/fund-usdc.sh` | Mints real USDC to test identities on the fork |\n| `packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol` | Contract tests |\n| `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` | Tip page (stats + feed + form) |\n| `packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/` | `JarStats`, `TipFeed`, `TipForm` |\n| `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts` | USDC ABI (+ Base TipJar for production) |\n| `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` | Targets Base fork locally, Base mainnet for the IPFS build |\n\n---\n\nBuilt with [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":83,"newStart":1,"newLines":131,"lines":["-# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2","+# 💸 USDC Tip Jar on Base"," ","-<h4 align=\"center\">","-  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |","-  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>","-</h4>","+A [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io) (Foundry) dApp that accepts **USDC tips on Base**.","+It ships two things:"," ","-🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.","+- **`TipJar` contract** — pulls USDC via `approve` + `transferFrom`, records every tip","+  (sender, amount, message, timestamp) in an on-chain feed, and lets the owner withdraw.","+- **Tip page** — jar stats, a live tip feed, and a form to approve + send a tip."," ","-> [!NOTE]","-> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.","+Base USDC: [`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`](https://basescan.org/address/0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913)"," ","-⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.","+The team develops and demos **locally against real Base state**: a Base mainnet fork served","+on a local anvil chain. Tips move the **real USDC contract's** balances between test","+identities, but nothing touches mainnet and **no real money is at risk**."," ","-- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.","-- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.","-- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.","-- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.","-- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.","+> Shipping the site? 2](https://scaffoldeth.io)."]}],"originalFile":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n\n```\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n\n4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n\n```\nyarn start\n```\n\nVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\nRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n\n- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMFvC8LjLSFSHWVHgLXZ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"`external` overrides `deployed` on merge — good to know for the docs. Now DEPLOY.md:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2019,"cache_read_input_tokens":135123,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2019,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"ff313db3-aadf-4d27-a367-ecc6e102fdcd","timestamp":"2026-07-24T17:14:32.787Z","request_id":"req_011CdMFvAStVNjS1PTKMYSFe"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMFvC8LjLSFSHWVHgLXZ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0192yjx2id3ttTVPEz1miVNd","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T165312Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/DEPLOY.md","content":"# Deploying the USDC Tip Jar (static site on IPFS)\n\nThe site ships as a **static export** hosted on IPFS. There is no server. This document\nlists the exact commands to **build**, **upload**, and **verify** a production deploy that\ntargets **Base mainnet**.\n\n> These commands publish a real site and (in step 0) a real contract that spends real ETH\n> for gas. Run them only when you actually intend to ship.\n\n---\n\n## Production build configuration (already wired)\n\n| File | Behaviour when `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true` |\n| --- | --- |\n| `packages/nextjs/next.config.ts` | `output: \"export\"`, `trailingSlash: true`, unoptimized images → static `out/` |\n| `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` | `targetNetworks = [base]` (chain id 8453) |\n| `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts` | Base USDC ABI + Base `TipJar` at `NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS` |\n\nTwo required inputs:\n\n- **Node 20 or 22.** The static export prerenders pages in Node; Node ≥ 23 crashes on a\n  global `localStorage` reference. Use `nvm use 22` before building.\n- **`NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS`** — the TipJar address on Base (from step 0). The build\n  bakes this in; the frontend reads it for chain id 8453.\n\n---\n\n## 0. Deploy `TipJar` to Base mainnet\n\n```bash\n# One-time: create (or import) a funded deployer keystore. Do NOT use the default account.\nyarn generate                 # or: yarn account:import\nyarn account                  # confirm the address is funded with Base ETH for gas\n\n# Deploy the jar (constructor uses canonical Base USDC).\nyarn deploy --file DeployTipJar.s.sol --network base\n```\n\nGrab the deployed address from the command output or from\n`packages/foundry/deployments/8453.json`, and export it for the build:\n\n```bash\nexport NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS=0x<your-deployed-tipjar-on-base>\n```\n\n---\n\n## 1. Build the static site (no upload)\n\n```bash\nnvm use 22\ncd packages/nextjs\nNEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS=$NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS yarn next:build\n```\n\nOutput: `packages/nextjs/out/` — a fully static, relative-path site (`index.html`,\n`_next/…`). Sanity-check it locally before uploading:\n\n```bash\ngrep -q \"USDC Tip Jar\" out/index.html && echo \"OK: homepage rendered\"\nnpx serve out            # optional: open http://localhost:3000 and click around\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Upload to IPFS\n\nThe repo bundles [`bgipfs`](https://bgipfs.com). Upload the already-built `out/`:\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nyarn bgipfs upload config init -u https://upload.bgipfs.com   # first time only\nyarn bgipfs upload out\n# → prints \"CID: <cid>\"; export it for verification:\nexport CID=<cid-from-output>\n```\n\nAlternatives (pick one, all produce a CID for the same `out/`):\n\n```bash\n# Kubo (local IPFS node)\nipfs add -r --cid-version 1 out\n\n# Pinning service (e.g. Pinata) via their CLI/API — pin the out/ directory\n```\n\n> `yarn ipfs` is an all-in-one shortcut that **builds *and* uploads** in one step\n> (`NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS=0x… yarn ipfs`). Use the split steps above when you want to\n> inspect the build before publishing.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Post-deploy verification\n\n**a. The site loads from a public gateway and is fully static:**\n\n```bash\ncurl -sL \"https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/$CID/\" | grep -q \"USDC Tip Jar\" && echo \"OK: served from IPFS\"\n# No absolute localhost/server URLs should appear in the export:\n! grep -rql \"http://localhost\" out/_next && echo \"OK: no localhost references\"\n```\n\nOpen `https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/$CID/` (or `ipfs://$CID`) in a browser.\n\n**b. The baked-in Base contracts are correct:**\n\n```bash\nBASE=https://mainnet.base.org\nUSDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\n\n# TipJar points at canonical Base USDC:\ncast call $NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS \"token()(address)\" --rpc-url $BASE   # → $USDC\ncast call $NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS \"owner()(address)\" --rpc-url $BASE   # → your deployer\n```\n\n**c. Verify the source on Basescan** (optional but recommended):\n\n```bash\nyarn verify --network base\n# or: forge verify-contract $NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS TipJar --chain base --watch\n```\n\n**d. End-to-end smoke test (spends a small real amount):** connect a Base wallet on the\nIPFS-hosted site, approve + send a minimal USDC tip, and confirm it appears in the feed and\nthat `cast call $NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS \"totalTipped()(uint256)\" --rpc-url $BASE`\nincreased.\n\n---\n\n## Notes\n\n- The Base `TipJar` address comes from `NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS` via\n  `externalContracts.ts`. Because external contracts override generated ones, this env var is\n  the single source of truth for the production address even if you also deployed from this\n  repo — always build with it set.\n- To roll a new frontend without redeploying the contract, rebuild (step 1) and re-upload\n  (step 2); the CID changes, the contract address does not.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2019,"cache_read_input_tokens":135123,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2019,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"883ada90-ae4c-4b43-b959-8195f7e5d7a6","timestamp":"2026-07-24T17:15:02.466Z","request_id":"req_011CdMFvAStVNjS1PTKMYSFe"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0192yjx2id3ttTVPEz1miVNd","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T165312Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/DEPLOY.md (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"8004a11a-35b1-40bc-92a5-f88b9d8cb0da","timestamp":"2026-07-24T17:15:02.474Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T165312Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/DEPLOY.md","content":"# Deploying the USDC Tip Jar (static site on IPFS)\n\nThe site ships as a **static export** hosted on IPFS. There is no server. This document\nlists the exact commands to **build**, **upload**, and **verify** a production deploy that\ntargets **Base mainnet**.\n\n> These commands publish a real site and (in step 0) a real contract that spends real ETH\n> for gas. Run them only when you actually intend to ship.\n\n---\n\n## Production build configuration (already wired)\n\n| File | Behaviour when `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true` |\n| --- | --- |\n| `packages/nextjs/next.config.ts` | `output: \"export\"`, `trailingSlash: true`, unoptimized images → static `out/` |\n| `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` | `targetNetworks = [base]` (chain id 8453) |\n| `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts` | Base USDC ABI + Base `TipJar` at `NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS` |\n\nTwo required inputs:\n\n- **Node 20 or 22.** The static export prerenders pages in Node; Node ≥ 23 crashes on a\n  global `localStorage` reference. Use `nvm use 22` before building.\n- **`NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS`** — the TipJar address on Base (from step 0). The build\n  bakes this in; the frontend reads it for chain id 8453.\n\n---\n\n## 0. Deploy `TipJar` to Base mainnet\n\n```bash\n# One-time: create (or import) a funded deployer keystore. Do NOT use the default account.\nyarn generate                 # or: yarn account:import\nyarn account                  # confirm the address is funded with Base ETH for gas\n\n# Deploy the jar (constructor uses canonical Base USDC).\nyarn deploy --file DeployTipJar.s.sol --network base\n```\n\nGrab the deployed address from the command output or from\n`packages/foundry/deployments/8453.json`, and export it for the build:\n\n```bash\nexport NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS=0x<your-deployed-tipjar-on-base>\n```\n\n---\n\n## 1. Build the static site (no upload)\n\n```bash\nnvm use 22\ncd packages/nextjs\nNEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS=$NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS yarn next:build\n```\n\nOutput: `packages/nextjs/out/` — a fully static, relative-path site (`index.html`,\n`_next/…`). Sanity-check it locally before uploading:\n\n```bash\ngrep -q \"USDC Tip Jar\" out/index.html && echo \"OK: homepage rendered\"\nnpx serve out            # optional: open http://localhost:3000 and click around\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Upload to IPFS\n\nThe repo bundles [`bgipfs`](https://bgipfs.com). Upload the already-built `out/`:\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nyarn bgipfs upload config init -u https://upload.bgipfs.com   # first time only\nyarn bgipfs upload out\n# → prints \"CID: <cid>\"; export it for verification:\nexport CID=<cid-from-output>\n```\n\nAlternatives (pick one, all produce a CID for the same `out/`):\n\n```bash\n# Kubo (local IPFS node)\nipfs add -r --cid-version 1 out\n\n# Pinning service (e.g. Pinata) via their CLI/API — pin the out/ directory\n```\n\n> `yarn ipfs` is an all-in-one shortcut that **builds *and* uploads** in one step\n> (`NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS=0x… yarn ipfs`). Use the split steps above when you want to\n> inspect the build before publishing.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Post-deploy verification\n\n**a. The site loads from a public gateway and is fully static:**\n\n```bash\ncurl -sL \"https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/$CID/\" | grep -q \"USDC Tip Jar\" && echo \"OK: served from IPFS\"\n# No absolute localhost/server URLs should appear in the export:\n! grep -rql \"http://localhost\" out/_next && echo \"OK: no localhost references\"\n```\n\nOpen `https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/$CID/` (or `ipfs://$CID`) in a browser.\n\n**b. The baked-in Base contracts are correct:**\n\n```bash\nBASE=https://mainnet.base.org\nUSDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\n\n# TipJar points at canonical Base USDC:\ncast call $NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS \"token()(address)\" --rpc-url $BASE   # → $USDC\ncast call $NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS \"owner()(address)\" --rpc-url $BASE   # → your deployer\n```\n\n**c. Verify the source on Basescan** (optional but recommended):\n\n```bash\nyarn verify --network base\n# or: forge verify-contract $NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS TipJar --chain base --watch\n```\n\n**d. End-to-end smoke test (spends a small real amount):** connect a Base wallet on the\nIPFS-hosted site, approve + send a minimal USDC tip, and confirm it appears in the feed and\nthat `cast call $NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS \"totalTipped()(uint256)\" --rpc-url $BASE`\nincreased.\n\n---\n\n## Notes\n\n- The Base `TipJar` address comes from `NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS` via\n  `externalContracts.ts`. Because external contracts override generated ones, this env var is\n  the single source of truth for the production address even if you also deployed from this\n  repo — always build with it set.\n- To roll a new frontend without redeploying the contract, rebuild (step 1) and re-upload\n  (step 2); the CID changes, the contract address does not.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

Use one of these for both `yarn start` and the IPFS build —\n  Node ≥ 23 crashes Next's server rendering with `localStorage.getItem is not a function`.\n- `git`","originalFile":"# 💸 USDC Tip Jar on Base\n\nA [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io) (Foundry) dApp that accepts **USDC tips on Base**.\nIt ships two things:\n\n- **`TipJar` contract** — pulls USDC via `approve` + `transferFrom`, records every tip\n  (sender, amount, message, timestamp) in an on-chain feed, and lets the owner withdraw.\n- **Tip page** — jar stats, a live tip feed, and a form to approve + send a tip.\n\nBase USDC: [`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`](https://basescan.org/address/0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913)\n\nThe team develops and demos **locally against real Base state**: a Base mainnet fork served\non a local anvil chain. Tips move the **real USDC contract's** balances between test\nidentities, but nothing touches mainnet and **no real money is at risk**.\n\n> Shipping the site? See **[DEPLOY.md](./DEPLOY.md)** for the static IPFS build, upload, and\n> post-deploy verification commands.\n\n---\n\n## Requirements\n\n- [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation) (`forge`, `anvil`, `cast`)\n- **Node 20 or 22** (the static IPFS export crashes on Node ≥ 23) and Yarn v3+\n- `git`\n\nInstall dependencies once:\n\n```bash\nyarn install\n```\n\n---\n\n## Local workflow (real Base state, no real money)\n\nRun each numbered step in its own terminal. Everything targets a Base **fork** at\n`http://127.0.0.1:8545` with chain id `31337`.\n\n### 1. Start a Base mainnet fork\n\n```bash\nyarn fork:base\n```\n\nThis runs `anvil --fork-url https://mainnet.base.org --chain-id 31337`. The fork has the\nreal Base USDC contract and all of its balances/logic — it just runs on your machine.\n\n> Prefer your own RPC (the public endpoint is rate-limited)? Pass one in:\n> `yarn workspace @se-2/foundry fork:base` with `BASE_RPC_URL` set, e.g.\n> `BASE_RPC_URL=https://base-mainnet.g.alchemy.com/v2/<key> yarn fork:base`.\n\n### 2. Deploy the TipJar to the fork\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy:fork\n```\n\nDeploys `TipJar(USDC, deployer)` and regenerates the frontend ABIs into\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`.\n\n> Why `deploy:fork` instead of `yarn deploy`? On a Base fork the default deployer account\n> already has a transaction history on real Base, so `forge` needs an explicit `--sender`\n> to read the right nonce. `yarn deploy:fork` passes it for you. (`yarn deploy` is still the\n> command for a fresh anvil chain or a live network.)\n\n### 3. Fund test identities with real USDC\n\n```bash\n# Fund the default anvil accounts (100 USDC each)\nyarn fund\n\n# Also fund a specific address — e.g. copy your in-app burner wallet address:\nyarn fund 0xYourBurnerAddress\n\n# Custom amount:\nAMOUNT=250 yarn fund 0xYourBurnerAddress\n```\n\n`yarn fund` impersonates USDC's on-chain `masterMinter` on the fork, registers a local\nminter, and mints real USDC to each recipient. No mainnet transaction is sent.\n\n### 4. Start the frontend\n\n```bash\nyarn start\n```\n\nOpen **http://localhost:3000**.\n\n### 5. Demo a tip\n\n1. Connect the **Burner Wallet** (shown automatically on the local network). Copy its\n   address and fund it: `yarn fund <burnerAddress>`.\n2. Enter an amount and a message, then **Send tip**. The first tip asks for two wallet\n   confirmations: one to `approve` USDC, one to `tip`.\n3. The tip appears in the feed and the jar stats update — real USDC just moved from your\n   test identity into the jar.\n4. To withdraw, connect the **owner** account (the deployer,\n   `0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720`) and call `withdraw()` from the\n   `Debug Contracts` page.\n\n---\n\n## Contract tests\n\n```bash\nyarn foundry:test\n```\n\nUnit tests use a 6-decimal mock USDC (no fork needed) and cover the tip/approve flow, the\nfeed ordering, event emission, access control, and withdrawal.\n\n---\n\n## Project layout\n\n| Path | What |\n| --- | --- |\n| `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol` | The tip jar contract |\n| `packages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol` | Deploy script (uses Base USDC, override with `USDC_ADDRESS`) |\n| `packages/foundry/scripts-js/fund-usdc.sh` | Mints real USDC to test identities on the fork |\n| `packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol` | Contract tests |\n| `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` | Tip page (stats + feed + form) |\n| `packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/` | `JarStats`, `TipFeed`, `TipForm` |\n| `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts` | USDC ABI (+ Base TipJar for production) |\n| `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` | Targets Base fork locally, Base mainnet for the IPFS build |\n\n---\n\nBuilt with [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":21,"oldLines":7,"newStart":21,"newLines":8,"lines":[" ## Requirements"," "," - [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation) (`forge`, `anvil`, `cast`)","-- **Node 20 or 22** (the static IPFS export crashes on Node ≥ 23) and Yarn v3+","+- **Node 20 or 22** and Yarn v3+. Use one of these for both `yarn start` and the IPFS build —","+  Node ≥ 23 crashes Next's server rendering with `localStorage.getItem is not a function`."," - `git`"," "," Install dependencies once:"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015z6FCwiG8Fq2ZDm8Hm1HN2","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tname: se2-node-static-export\n3\tdescription: Scaffold-ETH 2 Next.js static export (IPFS build) crashes on Node >=23 due to a global localStorage\n4\tmetadata: \n5\t  node_type: memory\n6\t  type: project\n7\t  originSessionId: 2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f\n8\t  modified: 2026-07-24T17:09:45.391Z\n9\t---\n10\t\n11\tIn the ethskills-evals SE-2 workspaces, `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build`\n12\t(Next.js static `output: \"export\"`) fails prerendering the stock `/_not-found`\n13\tpage with `TypeError: localStorage.getItem is not a function`.\n14\t\n15\t**Cause:** Node >=23 (env default here is 25.9.0) exposes a global `localStorage`\n16\tobject; wagmi/burner-connector code that guards with `typeof localStorage !==\n17\t\"undefined\"` then runs during SSR prerender and blows up. Node 20/22 have no\n18\tglobal `localStorage`, so the guard works.\n19\t\n20\t**Fix A (Node 20/22):** build with Node 20 or 22 LTS (`nvm use`, e.g. 22.2.0 is\n21\tinstalled and verified). Do NOT bake `--no-experimental-webstorage` into\n22\tNODE_OPTIONS — that flag errors on Node <22.4.\n23\t\n24\t**Fix B (works on Node 25, verified):** ship a `packages/nextjs/polyfill-localstorage.cjs`\n25\tthat reinstalls the WebStorage methods when a global `localStorage` exists but\n26\tlacks `getItem`, and inject it with `NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs\"`.\n27\tMust use `--require` (not `instrumentation.ts`) so it loads in every prerender\n28\tworker. Add the same NODE_OPTIONS to the nextjs `dev`/`start` scripts too —\n29\totherwise `next dev` SSR throws the same error on Node 25 (page still hydrates\n30\tclient-side, but client components fail to SSR). Polyfill is a no-op on Node 20/22.\n31\t\n32\tAlso: `scaffold.config.ts` `targetNetworks` must be a single concrete network\n33\t(compile-time value the contract types derive from). A runtime env ternary makes\n34\tit a union type and collapses `ContractName` to the intersection of both\n35\tnetworks' contracts, breaking type-check.\n36\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"3570de74-3e3c-4974-8bda-60681b97aff3","timestamp":"2026-07-24T17:18:36.109Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/.claude/projects/-Users-spencerfaber-dev-ethskills-evals/memory/se2-node-static-export.md","content":"---\nname: se2-node-static-export\ndescription: Scaffold-ETH 2 Next.js static export (IPFS build) crashes on Node >=23 due to a global localStorage\nmetadata: \n  node_type: memory\n  type: project\n  originSessionId: 2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f\n  modified: 2026-07-24T17:09:45.391Z\n---\n\nIn the ethskills-evals SE-2 workspaces, `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build`\n(Next.js static `output: \"export\"`) fails prerendering the stock `/_not-found`\npage with `TypeError: localStorage.getItem is not a function`.\n\n**Cause:** Node >=23 (env default here is 25.9.0) exposes a global `localStorage`\nobject; wagmi/burner-connector code that guards with `typeof localStorage !==\n\"undefined\"` then runs during SSR prerender and blows up. Node 20/22 have no\nglobal `localStorage`, so the guard works.\n\n**Fix A (Node 20/22):** build with Node 20 or 22 LTS (`nvm use`, e.g. 22.2.0 is\ninstalled and verified). Do NOT bake `--no-experimental-webstorage` into\nNODE_OPTIONS — that flag errors on Node <22.4.\n\n**Fix B (works on Node 25, verified):** ship a `packages/nextjs/polyfill-localstorage.cjs`\nthat reinstalls the WebStorage methods when a global `localStorage` exists but\nlacks `getItem`, and inject it with `NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs\"`.\nMust use `--require` (not `instrumentation.ts`) so it loads in every prerender\nworker. Add the same NODE_OPTIONS to the nextjs `dev`/`start` scripts too —\notherwise `next dev` SSR throws the same error on Node 25 (page still hydrates\nclient-side, but client components fail to SSR). Polyfill is a no-op on Node 20/22.\n\nAlso: `scaffold.config.ts` `targetNetworks` must be a single concrete network\n(compile-time value the contract types derive from). A runtime env ternary makes\nit a union type and collapses `ContractName` to the intersection of both\nnetworks' contracts, breaking type-check.\n","numLines":36,"startLine":1,"totalLines":36}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMGGaJtn155GRyc5NGUq","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PPihEF8yv9j9EaeT2R4Xm9","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/.claude/projects/-Users-spencerfaber-dev-ethskills-evals/memory/se2-node-static-export.md","old_string":"Also: `scaffold.config.ts` `targetNetworks` must be a single concrete network\n(compile-time value the contract types derive from). A runtime env ternary makes\nit a union type and collapses `ContractName` to the intersection of both\nnetworks' contracts, breaking type-check.","new_string":"Also: `scaffold.config.ts` `targetNetworks` must be a single concrete network\n(compile-time value the contract types derive from). A runtime env ternary makes\nit a union type and collapses `ContractName` to the intersection of both\nnetworks' contracts, breaking type-check.\n\nWorkaround that DOES let a build-time env ternary switch networks (verified,\ntip-jar goal): `targetNetworks = isIpfs ? ([chains.base] as const) : ([chains.foundry] as const)`\nkeeps each branch's chain-id literal, so `ContractName` stays valid AS LONG AS both\nchains declare the same contract names (put shared contracts under both chain ids in\nexternalContracts). Two gotchas: (1) do NOT annotate it `readonly [chains.Chain]` —\nthat widens `.id` to `number` and breaks `ContractName`. (2) The union breaks wagmi's\n`chains` type in `services/web3/wagmiConfig.tsx`; fix by annotating\n`export const enabledChains: readonly [Chain, ...Chain[]] = ...`. Also note\n`deepMergeContracts` lets externalContracts OVERRIDE deployedContracts for the same\nname+chain, so an env-driven external address wins over a generated one."},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1050,"cache_read_input_tokens":155146,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1050,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":9,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"aae86404-88f3-4aeb-a3b9-9f293dd60f30","timestamp":"2026-07-24T17:18:54.281Z","request_id":"req_011CdMGGYrLEm9s9KT7xtjgX"}

A runtime env ternary makes\nit a union type and collapses `ContractName` to the intersection of both\nnetworks' contracts, breaking type-check.","newString":"Also: `scaffold.config.ts` `targetNetworks` must be a single concrete network\n(compile-time value the contract types derive from). A runtime env ternary makes\nit a union type and collapses `ContractName` to the intersection of both\nnetworks' contracts, breaking type-check.\n\nWorkaround that DOES let a build-time env ternary switch networks (verified,\ntip-jar goal): `targetNetworks = isIpfs ? ([chains.base] as const) : ([chains.foundry] as const)`\nkeeps each branch's chain-id literal, so `ContractName` stays valid AS LONG AS both\nchains declare the same contract names (put shared contracts under both chain ids in\nexternalContracts). Two gotchas: (1) do NOT annotate it `readonly [chains.Chain]` —\nthat widens `.id` to `number` and breaks `ContractName`. (2) The union breaks wagmi's\n`chains` type in `services/web3/wagmiConfig.tsx`; fix by annotating\n`export const enabledChains: readonly [Chain, ...Chain[]] = ...`. Also note\n`deepMergeContracts` lets externalContracts OVERRIDE deployedContracts for the same\nname+chain, so an env-driven external address wins over a generated one.","originalFile":"---\nname: se2-node-static-export\ndescription: Scaffold-ETH 2 Next.js static export (IPFS build) crashes on Node >=23 due to a global localStorage\nmetadata: \n  node_type: memory\n  type: project\n  originSessionId: 2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f\n  modified: 2026-07-24T17:09:45.391Z\n---\n\nIn the ethskills-evals SE-2 workspaces, `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build`\n(Next.js static `output: \"export\"`) fails prerendering the stock `/_not-found`\npage with `TypeError: localStorage.getItem is not a function`.\n\n**Cause:** Node >=23 (env default here is 25.9.0) exposes a global `localStorage`\nobject; wagmi/burner-connector code that guards with `typeof localStorage !==\n\"undefined\"` then runs during SSR prerender and blows up. Node 20/22 have no\nglobal `localStorage`, so the guard works.\n\n**Fix A (Node 20/22):** build with Node 20 or 22 LTS (`nvm use`, e.g. 22.2.0 is\ninstalled and verified). Do NOT bake `--no-experimental-webstorage` into\nNODE_OPTIONS — that flag errors on Node <22.4.\n\n**Fix B (works on Node 25, verified):** ship a `packages/nextjs/polyfill-localstorage.cjs`\nthat reinstalls the WebStorage methods when a global `localStorage` exists but\nlacks `getItem`, and inject it with `NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs\"`.\nMust use `--require` (not `instrumentation.ts`) so it loads in every prerender\nworker. Add the same NODE_OPTIONS to the nextjs `dev`/`start` scripts too —\notherwise `next dev` SSR throws the same error on Node 25 (page still hydrates\nclient-side, but client components fail to SSR). Polyfill is a no-op on Node 20/22.\n\nAlso: `scaffold.config.ts` `targetNetworks` must be a single concrete network\n(compile-time value the contract types derive from). A runtime env ternary makes\nit a union type and collapses `ContractName` to the intersection of both\nnetworks' contracts, breaking type-check.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":5,"oldLines":7,"newStart":5,"newLines":7,"lines":["   node_type: memory","   type: project","   originSessionId: 2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","-  modified: 2026-07-24T17:09:45.391Z","+  modified: 2026-07-24T17:18:54.287Z"," ---"," "," In the ethskills-evals SE-2 workspaces, `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build`"]},{"oldStart":33,"oldLines":3,"newStart":33,"newLines":14,"lines":[" (compile-time value the contract types derive from). A runtime env ternary makes"," it a union type and collapses `ContractName` to the intersection of both"," networks' contracts, breaking type-check.","+","+Workaround that DOES let a build-time env ternary switch networks (verified,","+tip-jar goal): `targetNetworks = isIpfs ? ([chains.base] as const) : ([chains.foundry] as const)`","+keeps each branch's chain-id literal, so `ContractName` stays valid AS LONG AS both","+chains declare the same contract names (put shared contracts under both chain ids in","+externalContracts). Two gotchas: (1) do NOT annotate it `readonly [chains.Chain]` —","+that widens `.id` to `number` and breaks `ContractName`. (2) The union breaks wagmi's","+`chains` type in `services/web3/wagmiConfig.tsx`; fix by annotating","+`export const enabledChains: readonly [Chain, ...Chain[]] = ...`. Also note","+`deepMergeContracts` lets externalContracts OVERRIDE deployedContracts for the same","+name+chain, so an env-driven external address wins over a generated one."]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false,"memdirStamped":true}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMGJie189Cpq5Y8sChKk","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Everything is built and verified. Here's the summary.\n\n## USDC Tip Jar on Base — done\n\n**Contract** (`packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`)\n- Accepts USDC tips via `approve` + `transferFrom` (SafeERC20), records each tip `{from, amount, message, timestamp}` in an on-chain feed, tracks totals, and lets the owner `withdraw()`. `recentTips(n)` returns the feed newest-first.\n- Constructor takes the token address; the deploy script wires in Base USDC `0x8335…2913` (override via `USDC_ADDRESS`).\n- 8 Foundry tests pass (approve/tip flow, feed ordering, event, access control, withdrawal) using a 6-decimal mock USDC.\n\n**Frontend** (`packages/nextjs`)\n- New tip page: `JarStats`, `TipFeed` (reads `recentTips` from chain state — avoids scanning millions of Base fork blocks for logs), and `TipForm` (handles the approve→tip two-step). USDC registered in `externalContracts.ts` for both chain ids.\n- `scaffold.config.ts` targets the local Base fork for dev and Base mainnet for the IPFS build.\n\n**Local workflow** (documented in `README.md`, all commands run and verified):\n- `yarn fork:base` → `yarn deploy:fork` → `yarn fund [addr]` → `yarn start`. I ran the full loop on a real Base fork and confirmed via `cast` that a tip moves real USDC into the jar, appears in the feed, and `withdraw()` moves it to the owner. `yarn fund` mints real USDC by impersonating USDC's on-chain `masterMinter` — no real money at risk.\n\n**Static IPFS build** (`DEPLOY.md`, verified):\n- `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build` produces `out/` (verified `index.html` renders \"USDC Tip Jar\"). DEPLOY.md gives exact Base-deploy, build, upload (`bgipfs`/`ipfs`), and post-deploy verification commands. Nothing was uploaded.\n\n**Notable things I had to solve (and documented):**\n- The default deployer is an *active* address on real Base, so stock `yarn deploy` fails with \"nonce too low\" on a fork — added `yarn deploy:fork` which passes an explicit `--sender`.\n- **Node ≥23 crashes both `yarn start` (dev SSR) and the IPFS export** with `localStorage.getItem is not a function` — README requires Node 20/22; I built and ran everything on 22.\n- Making `targetNetworks` a build-time ternary needed care to keep `ContractName` types and wagmi's `chains` tuple type valid; fixed in `scaffold.config.ts` + a one-line annotation in `wagmiConfig.tsx`, plus a latent `publicClient` null-guard in `useTransactor.tsx`. Type-check is clean (0 errors).\n\nBackground dev server and fork were shut down after verification.\n\nOne caveat worth flagging: I could validate the on-chain flow and that the dev server renders the UI, but I couldn't drive a browser wallet headlessly — the in-browser click-through (connect burner → approve → tip) is the one step you'd want to eyeball manually."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":227,"cache_read_input_tokens":159119,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":227,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ab73fb6-af66-41f3-9aaa-de656f12705b","uuid":"eca8afc5-f8b2-49c3-a0a6-907c5f77cab0","timestamp":"2026-07-24T17:19:22.490Z","request_id":"req_011CdMGJgznJXxfkAH21vVpv"}
